```
7. 月总后:无(事件域已审)
8. 推进公告/成就/难度旗标各节点
9. 困难模式敌人替换表(地表/洞穴 hardmode 怪池切换)——刷怪域已核表,只确认切换点
```

## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T01:02:42.472Z

**📎 ToolResult**

```
	public static void StartHardmode(bool force = false)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 1 || (Main.hardMode && !force))
		{
			return;
		}
		Main.hardMode = true;
		TryProtectingSpawnedItems();
		TransformWorldOnBackgroundThread(initializeHardMode, delegate
		{
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(NetworkText.FromKey(Lang.misc[15].Key), ChatColors.World);
			AchievementsHelper.NotifyProgressionEvent(9);
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				Netplay.ResetSections();
			}
			UndoSpawnedItemProtection();
		});
	}

	public static void TransformWorldOnBackgroundThread(Action transform, Action mainThreadFollowup)
	{
		Interlocked.Increment(ref _transformingWorld);
		Task.Factory.StartNew(delegate
		{
			try
			{
				lock (WorldFile.IOLock)
				{
					transform();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				Interlocked.Decrement(ref _transformingWorld);
				if (mainThreadFollowup != null)
				{
					Main.QueueMainThreadAction(mainThreadFollowup);
				}
			}
		});
	}

	public static void ClearUnbreakableWallsWithPaintUpTo(int tierColor)
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		TransformWorldOnBackgroundThread(delegate
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < Main.maxTilesX; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < Main.maxTilesY; j++)
				{
					Tile tile = Main.tile[i, j];
					if (tile.wall == 350 && (tile.wallColor() & 0xF) <= tierColor)
					{
						tile.wall = TileID.Sets.MatchingUnsafeWall[tile.type];
						tile.wallColor(0);
					}
				}
			}
		}, delegate
		{
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				Netplay.ResetSections();
			}
		});
	}
```

```
135	/** initializeHardMode（cs:31734-31860）1:1（remix/dualDungeons/skyblock 分支不适用） */
136	export function initializeHardMode(world: World, rng: RNG): void {
137	  const st = world.store, w = st.w, h = st.h;
138	  // ---- V 带位置（cs:31740-31781）----
139	  const num = rng.nextIntRange(300, 400) * 0.001;        // 神圣带侧比例
140	  const num2 = rng.nextIntRange(200, 300) * 0.001;       // 邪恶带侧比例
141	  let num3 = Math.trunc(w * num);                        // 神圣带 x
142	  let num4 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num));                  // 邪恶带 x
143	  let num5 = 1;
144	  if (rng.nextInt(2) === 0) {
145	    num4 = Math.trunc(w * num);
146	    num3 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num));
147	    num5 = -1;
148	  }
149	  const num6 = world.dungeonX < w / 2 ? -1 : 1;          // 神圣带放地牢对侧
150	  if (num6 < 0) {
151	    if (num4 < num3) num4 = Math.trunc(w * num2);
152	    else num3 = Math.trunc(w * num2);
153	  } else if (num4 > num3) num4 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num2));
154	  else num3 = Math.trunc(w * (1 - num2));
155	  geRunner(st, rng, num3, 0, 3 * num5, 5.0, true, world.crimson);
156	  geRunner(st, rng, num4, 0, -3 * num5, 5.0, false, world.crimson);
157	  // ---- 洞穴墙回填（cs:31783-31860）：25×(w/4200) 片 ----
158	  let budget = Math.trunc(25 * (w / 4200));
159	  let attempts = 0;
160	  while (budget > 0) {
161	    if (++attempts % 15000 === 0) budget--;              // 原版放弃退减（防死循环）
162	    // RandomWorldPoint(worldSurface-100, 1, 190, 1)：x∈[1,w-2]、y∈[ws-100,h-191]
163	    const px = rng.nextIntRange(1, w - 1);
164	    const py = rng.nextIntRange(Math.max(20, Math.trunc(world.groundLevel) - 100), h - 190);
165	    if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;
166	    const ii = st.idx(px, py);
167	    const sh = TILE_DEFS[st.type[ii]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
168	    let wallId = 0;
169	    if (SET_CRIMSON.has(sh)) wallId = 192 + rng.nextInt(4);       // 猩红墙 192-195
170	    else if (SET_CORRUPT.has(sh)) wallId = 188 + rng.nextInt(4);  // 腐化墙 188-191
171	    else if (SET_HALLOW.has(sh)) wallId = 200 + rng.nextInt(4);   // 神圣墙 200-203
172	    if (st.flags[ii] && wallId !== 0 && !st.flags[st.idx(px, py - 1)]) {
173	      // ShapeFloodFill(1000)：非实心 + OnlyWalls 白名单泛洪；OuterOutline(+对角+内腔)
174	      const cells = new Set<number>();
175	      let complete = true;
176	      const queue: Array<[number, number]> = [[px, py - 1]];
177	      let quota = 1000;
178	      while (queue.length && quota > 0) {
179	        const [qx, qy] = queue.shift()!;
180	        const qi = st.idx(qx, qy);
181	        if (cells.has(qi)) continue;
182	        if (st.flags[qi]) continue;                      // IsNotSolid
183	        if (!BACKFILL_WALLS.has(st.wall[qi])) continue;  // OnlyWalls
184	        cells.add(qi);
185	        quota--;
186	        if (qx + 1 < w - 1) queue.push([qx + 1, qy]);
187	        if (qx - 1 >= 1) queue.push([qx - 1, qy]);
188	        if (qy + 1 < h - 1) queue.push([qx, qy + 1]);
189	        if (qy - 1 >= 1) queue.push([qx, qy - 1]);
190	      }
191	      if (quota <= 0 && queue.length) complete = false;
192	      if (cells.size > 50 && complete) {
193	        for (const ci of cells) {                        // 腔体 + 8 邻环铺墙
194	          const cx = ci % st.w, cy = Math.trunc(ci / st.w);
195	          const paint = (wx: number, wy: number): void => {
196	            if (st.inBounds(wx, wy)) st.setWall(wx, wy, wallId);
197	          };
198	          paint(cx + 1, cy); paint(cx - 1, cy); paint(cx, cy + 1); paint(cx, cy - 1);
199	          paint(cx + 1, cy + 1); paint(cx + 1, cy - 1); paint(cx - 1, cy + 1); paint(cx - 1, cy - 1);
200	          paint(cx, cy);
201	        }
202	        budget--;
203	      }
204	    }
205	  }
206	}
207	
208	/** CreateBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh（NPC.cs:79837-79866）：击杀点空心砖盒+清岩浆。
209	 *  cxTile/cyTile = 击杀 NPC 中心格；widthPx = 碰撞盒宽。盒半径 = (int)(width/2/16)+1
210	 *  （C# 整除链：100/2=50 → 50/16=3 → +1=4） */
211	export function createBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh(st: TileStore, cxTile: number, cyTile: number, widthPx: number, crimson: boolean): void {
212	  const r = Math.trunc(widthPx / 2 / 16) + 1;
213	  const brick = crimson ? id(347) : id(140);             // 猩红砖 347 / 魔金砖 140
214	  for (let i = cxTile - r; i <= cxTile + r; i++) {
215	    for (let j = cyTile - r; j <= cyTile + r; j++) {
216	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
217	      const ii = st.idx(i, j);
218	      const onShell = i === cxTile - r || i === cxTile + r || j === cyTile - r || j === cyTile + r;
219	      if (onShell && !st.flags[ii]) {
220	        st.setTile(i, j, brick);
221	      }
222	      st.liquid[ii] = 0; st.liquidType[ii] = 0;          // 盒内岩浆清零
223	    }
224	  }
225	}
226	
227	/** StartHardmode（cs:31866-31880）+ SetEventFlagCleared（NPC.cs:80281-80292）语义。
228	 *  调用方（Game 击杀流）按原版顺序执行：brickbox → 捕获旧 hardMode → 本函数 →
229	 *  (!wasHard 时) 灯笼夜 19 → misc[15] 公告 + 成就 9（由返回值委托，调用方消费）。 */
230	export function startHardmode(world: World): { wasHard: boolean } {
231	  const wasHard = !!world.flags.hardMode;
232	  (world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).hardMode = true;
233	  initializeHardMode(world, new RNG(world.seed));
234	  return { wasHard };
235	}
236	

```

```
  /** 砸祭坛 = 原版 KillTile case 26 → SmashAltar（WorldGen.cs:48949）1:1：
   *  altarCount%3 选矿档(0 钴/钯、1 秘银/山铜、2 精金/钛)，/3+1 为波次衰减；
   *  矿脉数 num3 = w/4200×310 − 85×档 ×0.85 ÷ 波次——钴档恒 ×1.05（:49008），
   *  锁定到替代矿(221/222/223)再 ×0.9（:48991 等,替代矿更稀有）；
   *  矿种按世界锁定（SavedOreTiers :48981-49030：每档首砸 roll 一次 genRand.Next(2)==0
   *  → 替代矿,此后全世界固定,随存档持久化 world.savedOreTiers——此前每砸重 roll
   *  会导致同世界混出钴+钯）；深度带按锁定矿种（:49033-49040：107/221=地表线、
   *  108/222=岩层、111/223=(岩层×2+h)/3）；每脉 OreRunner Next(5,10) 恒定（:49045,
   *  旧实现的 5..9+w/4200 是误读）;最后生成 1-2 只幽灵(82)。祭坛 3×2 整体移除。
   *  计数存 world.altarCount（原版 WorldGen.altarCount 静态位，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100
   *  随存档持久化——跨会话保留；海盗入侵自然 roll 的 >0 门同源 Main.cs:64938） */
  private smashAltar(tx: number, ty: number): void {
    const st = this.world.store;
    const ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_26_altars'];
    // 3×2 对象拆除（TileObjectData 语义）：frameX = style×54 + 列×18 → 列号 %3;
    // frameY/18∈{0,1} 行。锚点 = 左上格——此前扫 ±3 邻域会误拆 3 格内相邻祭坛
    const aIdx = st.idx(tx, ty);
    const ax = tx - (Math.floor(st.frameX[aIdx] / 18) % 3);
    const ay = ty - Math.floor(st.frameY[aIdx] / 18);
    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
      for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
        if (!st.inBounds(ax + dx, ay + dy)) continue;
        if (st.type[st.idx(ax + dx, ay + dy)] === ALTAR) st.setTileSilent(ax + dx, ay + dy, 0);
      }
    }
    this.chunks.markDirtyArea(ax - 1, ay - 1, ax + 3, ay + 2);
    this.spawnParticles(tx * TILE + 24, ty * TILE + 8, '#B080FF', 14, 2.5);
    this.sfx.play('hit', 1);
    const tier = this.world.altarCount % 3;
    const wave = Math.floor(this.world.altarCount / 3) + 1;
    // 世界锁定选种（SavedOreTiers）：每档 base/alt 二选一,首砸 roll 后固定
    const TIER_ORES: Array<{ slot: 'cobalt' | 'mythril' | 'adamantite'; base: number; alt: number }> = [
      { slot: 'cobalt', base: 107, alt: 221 },
      { slot: 'mythril', base: 108, alt: 222 },
      { slot: 'adamantite', base: 111, alt: 223 },
    ];
    const td = TIER_ORES[tier];
    if (this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot] === -1) {
      this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot] = Math.random() < 0.5 ? td.base : td.alt; // Next(2)==0 → 替代
    }
    const oreSheet = this.world.savedOreTiers[td.slot];
    const ore = TILE_INTERNAL_BY_SHEET.get(oreSheet) ?? TILE_BY_KEY['ore_cobalt'];
    // 世界公告（:48991/:49008/:49021——ChatHelper misc[12/13/14 基础,+9=替代矿变体],
    // 按锁定矿种选文案）
    this.newText(Lang.misc((tier === 0 ? 12 : tier === 1 ? 13 : 14) + (oreSheet !== td.base ? 9 : 0)), 175, 75, 255);
    let veins = (st.w / 4200) * 310 - 85 * tier;
    veins *= 0.85;
    veins /= wave;
    if (tier === 0) veins *= 1.05;              // 钴档恒 ×1.05（:49008）
    if (oreSheet !== td.base) veins *= 0.9;     // 替代矿更稀有（:48991/:49016/:49029）
    const band = tier === 0 ? this.world.groundLevel : tier === 1 ? this.world.rockLevel : (this.world.rockLevel * 2 + st.h) / 3;
    const rng = new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0);
    const cap = Math.floor(veins);
    for (let k = 0; k < cap; k++) {
      const x = 100 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (st.w - 200));
      const y = Math.floor(band) + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, st.h - 150 - Math.floor(band)));
      const r = rng.int(5, 9);   // Next(5,10) 恒定区间(:49045)——旧 5..9+w/4200 系误读
      tileRunner(st, rng, lastGenState() ?? { worldSurface: this.world.groundLevel, lavaLine: st.h - 200, waterLine: this.world.groundLevel } as never, x, y, { strength: r, steps: r, type: ore, addTile: true });
    }
    const wraiths = 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2);
    for (let k = 0; k < wraiths; k++) {
      const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(82, this.player.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 64, this.player.y - 32);
      if (e) {
        e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
        this.entities.enemies.push(e);
      }
    }
    this.world.altarCount++;
    // 成就：BEGONE_EVIL（WorldGen.cs:49111 event 6——每次砸祭坛都触发）
    this.achievements.notifyProgressionEvent(6);
  }

  private tryMine(tx: number, ty: number, power: number, toolType: 'pick' | 'axe' | 'hammer', speed: number, useTime?: number) {
    // 联机破坏保护（v3 房间制）：非房主禁挖/禁锤墙
    if (!this.netCanEditTile()) { this.mining = null; this.netDeny(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.RoomProtectTiles')); return; }
    // 范围检查
    if (!this.inTileRange(tx, ty)) { this.mining = null; return; }

    // 恶魔祭坛（tile 26，原版 Player.cs:45058）：镐不可破坏（def pick:-1 拦）；
    // 锤 ≥80 锤力且困难模式 → SmashAltar（砸碎=困难矿三档轮换+幽灵）；
    // 否则进度清零 + 玩家受 statLife/2 电击（ByOther(4)）
    if (type === TILE_BY_KEY['v_26_altars']) {
      this.mining = null;
      if (toolType === 'hammer' && power >= 80 && this.world.flags.hardMode) {
        this.smashAltar(tx, ty);
      } else if (toolType === 'hammer') {
        const p = this.player;
        const dmg = Math.max(1, Math.floor(p.maxHp / 2));
        p.damage(dmg, p.cx - p.facing * 8, p.cy, false);
        this.addDamageNumber(p.cx, p.y, dmg, false, '#B080FF');
        this.sfx.play('hit', 0.8);
      }
      return;
    }
    // 锤：敲除背景墙（无墙则无事发生；也可清装饰）
    if (toolType === 'hammer') {
      // 矿车轨道锤击（Player.cs:45442-45447）：FrameTrack(pound) 循环切换连接/加速向
      if (type === this.trackTile && this.trackTile !== 0) {
        if (this.tickCount - this.lastMineHitTick >= Math.max(8, speed)) {
          this.lastMineHitTick = this.tickCount;
          frameTrack(this.world.store, tx, ty, true);
          this.sfx.play('tink');
        }
        this.mining = null;
        return;
      }

      // 平台族的锤循环（:45394-45440 平台坡面/楼梯）未接入——依赖登记：
      // 平台 slope 帧的楼梯绘制 + 对应坡面碰撞（平台 slope1-4 的可行走楼梯几何）
      const hd = TILE_DEFS[type];
      const poundable = type !== 0 && !!hd && hd.solid && !hd.platform && !hd.framed;
```

```
            const flag = n === 1 ? 'downed_125' : n === 2 ? 'downed_126' : 'downed_127';
            if (!w.flags[flag] || bossesKeepSpawning) {
              this.spawnHardBossPending = n;

              break;
            }
          }
```

```
1:# Plantera's Bulb
3:> 原页面: `Plantera's_Bulb`
5:# Plantera's Bulb
10:Plantera's Bulb
22:Plantera's Bulb in dark and light.
24:**Plantera's Bulb** is a [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode") [background object](Background_object "Background object") that will summon [Plantera](Plantera.md "Plantera") when broken. Plantera's Bulbs are usually found naturally placed in random [Underground Jungle](Underground_Jungle.md "Underground Jungle") locations once all three of the [mechanical bosses](Mechanical_bosses.md "Mechanical bosses") have been defeated (on the [Nintendo 3DS](Legacy%3A3DS_version "Legacy:3DS version")[](Legacy%3A3DS_version "3DS")[ version](Legacy%3A3DS_version "Legacy:3DS version"), only one is required) – the [status message](Status_message "Status message") _" The jungle grows restless..."_ indicates that Plantera's Bulbs have begun spawning. On the [ Desktop version](Desktop_version_history.md "Desktop version history"), [ Console version](Console_version.md "Console version"), and [ Mobile version](Mobile_version.md "Mobile version"), one Plantera's Bulb will immediately spawn when all three mechanical bosses are defeated for the first time. 
26:Plantera's Bulbs emit faint pink light, and will appear on the [minimap](Minimap.md "Minimap") as pink in areas the player has already explored on the [ Desktop version](Desktop_version_history.md "Desktop version history"), [ Console version](Console_version.md "Console version"), and [ Mobile version](Mobile_version.md "Mobile version"). They will also be labeled as "Plantera's Bulb" if the cursor is hovered over them on the map screen. 
28:The bulbs spawn one at a time after a delay of about half a day between them, and unless destroyed, several will eventually be present (10–15 in a [large world's](Map_size "Map size") naturally generated Underground Jungle). If destroyed, they respawn after the same delay (though not necessarily in the same spot), allowing the player to summon Plantera an infinite number of times. Plantera's Bulbs cannot be detected by the [Metal Detector](Metal_Detector.md "Metal Detector")**** __(Desktop, Console and Mobile versions) or its upgrades. 
34:Plantera's Bulb on the map screen.
36:  * A Plantera's Bulb can be destroyed using the following methods: 
42:  * Plantera's Bulbs can spawn on any block of [Jungle grass](Jungle_grass.md "Jungle grass") (including player-placed ones) below the [Surface](Surface.md "Surface"); it does not have to be in an Underground Jungle biome. However, they will never spawn within 302×302-tiles rectangles (centered on the Plantera's Bulbs) of each other,[1] i.e. they are spaced 150 tiles apart at maximum concentration. 
43:    * Plantera's Bulbs can spawn in front of [background walls](Background_walls.md "Background walls").
44:    * Plantera's Bulbs can replace all forms of [wild Jungle plants](Grasses#Wild_plants_on_grass "Grasses") that grow on [Jungle grass](Jungle_grass.md "Jungle grass"), including [Jungle Spores](Jungle_Spores.md "Jungle Spores") and the common 3-block-wide bushes, so clearing plants to allow growth is not required.[2] The same is true of Jungle vines and thorns. However, Plantera's Bulbs do not replace [Bee Hives](Bee_Hive.md "Bee Hive").
45:  * Plantera will only be spawned if there is any player within a rhombus of which its diagonals are 50 tiles long, centered on the broken Plantera's Bulb.[3]
46:  * If a Plantera's Bulb is broken while Plantera is still alive, a second Plantera will not spawn.
50:  * If planning to farm Plantera, a viable method is by digging long tunnels of [Mud Blocks](Mud_Block.md "Mud Block") with Jungle grass, near an arena. 
52:    * [Sleeping](Sleeping.md "Sleeping")**** __(Desktop, Console and Mobile versions) is a good way to get Plantera's Bulbs to spawn.
53:  * In [Journey Mode](Journey_Mode.md "Journey Mode")**** __(Desktop, Console and Mobile versions) , the time speed can be set to 24× to increase the rate at which Plantera's Bulbs grow.
57:  * Plantera's Bulbs can spawn on blocks that are at 0' [depth](Depth "Depth"), giving the appearance of spawning in the surface layer.
63:  * [Desktop 1.4.4](Desktop_1.4.4 "Desktop 1.4.4"): The game will now try to spawn a Plantera's Bulb immediately when all [mechanical bosses](Mechanical_bosses.md "Mechanical bosses") are defeated for the first time.
```

```
const sheetOf = (id: number): number => TILE_DEFS[id]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
// 保底球茎 forceBulb 计分黑名单（WorldGen.cs:74266-74281，sheet=原版 id）：
// IsAContainer（BasicChest{21,88,467} 近似）+ 蜥蜴砖/祭坛/门/各结构块/轨道/巨石。
// Paintings 近似取 242-244，Boulders 取 379（TileID.Sets 两集合的已知成员）
const BURST_BLACKLIST = new Set<number>([
  21, 88, 467,            // IsAContainer
  226, 237, 10, 11, 26, 16, 15, 14, 104, 158, 105, 531, 349, 137, 443, 12, 314,
  242, 243, 244,          // TileID.Sets.Paintings 近似
  379,                    // TileID.Sets.Boulders
]);

export interface EvolutionHost {
  store: TileStore;
  worldSurface: number;
  rockLevel: number;
  /** Main.dungeonX（保底灯泡搜索框用，GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated cs:74182） */
  dungeonX: number;
  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
  crimson: boolean;
  isDay: boolean;
  raining: boolean;
  maxRaining: number;
  windSpeedCurrent: number;
  /** 玩家 tile 坐标（LOS 近似用；null = 无玩家） */
  playerTile: () => { x: number; y: number } | null;
}

export class WorldEvolution {
  private hardMode = false;
  private allowedToSpread = true;

  constructor(private host: EvolutionHost) {}

  /** 每 tick 调用（Game.fixedUpdate）。原版 UpdateWorld 采样骨架（cs:71507-71639） */
  update(): void {
    const st = this.host.store;
    const { w, h } = st;
    this.hardMode = !!this.host.flags.hardMode;
    this.allowedToSpread = true; // CreativePowers.StopBiomeSpread 无对应系统
    const rate = 1; // desiredWorldTilesUpdateRate = dayRate = 1（Main.cs:6290；睡觉 ×5 未接）
    const wallDist = 3;
    // 地表采样（雨天 ×1.5，cs:71552-71557）
    let surfaceN = w * h * 3e-5 * rate;
    if (this.host.raining) surfaceN *= 1.5;
    const num7 = 151 + (151 * 2.8 - 151) * Math.min(1, Math.max(0, w / 4200 - 1));
    for (let n = 0; n < surfaceN; n++) {
      if (Math.random() * (num7 * 100) < 1) this.plantAlch();
      const i = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (w - 20));
      const j = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) - 1 - 10));
      if (j < 10) continue;
      this.overgroundTile(i, j, wallDist);
    }
    // 地下采样（cs:71624-71633）
    const underN = w * h * 1.5e-5 * rate;
    for (let n = 0; n < underN; n++) {
      const i = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (w - 20));
      const j = Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) - 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (h - 20 - (Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface) - 1)));
      if (j < 10 || j >= h - 20) continue;
      this.undergroundTile(i, j, wallDist);
    }
  }

  // ================= 通用读写助手 =================

  private at(x: number, y: number) { return this.host.store.idx(x, y); }
  private active(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    return st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
  }
  private typeAt(x: number, y: number): number {
    const st = this.host.store;
    return st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.type[st.idx(x, y)] : -1;
  }
  private isLava(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    return st.inBounds(x, y) && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] === 2;
  }
  private liquidAt(x: number, y: number): number {
    const st = this.host.store;
    return st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] : 0;
  }
  private setType(x: number, y: number, type: number): void {
    const st = this.host.store;
    if (st.inBounds(x, y)) st.setTile(x, y, type);
  }
  private kill(x: number, y: number): void { this.setType(x, y, 0); }
  /** WorldGen.SolidTile：active && tileSolid && !solidTop && !half && slope==0 */
  private solidTile(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
    const i = st.idx(x, y);
    if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
    return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
  }
  /** PlayerLOS 近似：50 格内有玩家视为可见（原版逐玩家 Collision.CanHitLine 扫描） */
  private playerLOS(x: number, y: number): boolean {
    const p = this.host.playerTile();
    if (!p) return false;
    return Math.abs(p.x - x) < 50 && Math.abs(p.y - y) < 40;
  }
  private mechAny(): boolean {
    const f = this.host.flags;
    return !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']);
  }
  private mechAll(): boolean {
    const f = this.host.flags;
    // 原版 downedMechBoss1/2/3 = 毁灭者(134)/双子(125)/机械骷髅王(127)
    return !!(f['downed_134'] && f['downed_125'] && f['downed_127']);
  }

  /** 放置 1×1 装饰（帧 style×18,0；目标须为空——PlaceTile EmptyTile 门） */
  private place1(x: number, y: number, sheet: number, style = 0, variants = 1): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || this.active(x, y)) return false;
    const t = S(sheet);
    if (!t) return false;
    st.setTile(x, y, t, (style + (variants > 1 ? Math.floor(Math.random() * variants) : 0)) * 18, 0);
    return true;
  }
  /** 放置 w×h 整件（帧 dx*18, dy*18；区域全空才放）。styleX=样式基偏移（X 轴，
   *  如 2×2 横排样式族 style*36，TileObjectData StyleHorizontal=true） */
  private placeBlock(x: number, y: number, sheet: number, w: number, h: number, styleX = 0): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || this.active(x + dx, y + dy)) return false;
    }
    const t = S(sheet);
    if (!t) return false;
    for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
      st.setTile(x + dx, y + dy, t, styleX + dx * 18, dy * 18);
    }
    return true;
  }

  // ================= 保底世花球茎（GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated） =================

  /** WorldGen.GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated（cs:74180-74248）+ AttemptTo
   *  GeneratePlanteraBulbAt（cs:74250-74329）：三王全灭时在丛林侧搜索框
   *  [worldSurface, UnderworldLayer) 内掷 2500 点，逐点向上走找丛林草 60
   *  （<500 次后 forceBulb 允许泥 59，<200 次后允许非活动格）；找到即试
   *  (X,Y-1)/(X-1,Y-1)/(X+1,Y-1) 三锚，**恰好生成一个**灯泡即返回。 */
  planteraBulbBurst(): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    const { w } = st;
    const jungleLeft = this.host.dungeonX > w * 0.5;   // 丛林在地牢对侧（cs:74182）
    const x0 = Math.floor(jungleLeft ? w * 0.15 : w * 0.65);
    const x1 = Math.floor(jungleLeft ? w * 0.35 : w * 0.85);
    const yTop = Math.floor(this.host.worldSurface);
    const yBot = st.h - 200;                            // Main.UnderworldLayer（Main.cs:2863）
    let tries = 2500;
    while (tries > 0) {
      const forceBulb = tries < 500;                     // cs:74197 允许泥 59+计分档
      const allowInactive = tries < 200;                 // cs:74198
      tries--;
      let px = x0 + Math.floor(Math.random() * (x1 - x0));
      let py = yTop + Math.floor(Math.random() * (yBot - yTop));
      // 逐点向上走（≤500 步,cs:74206-74230）：flag2 时非活动格也算；命中 60/59 停
      let foundSheet = -1;
      let steps = 500;
      while (steps-- > 0) {
        py--;
        if (py < yTop) break;
        const sheet = sheetOf(this.typeAt(px, py));
        const act = this.active(px, py);
        if (allowInactive || act) {
          if (sheet === 60 || (forceBulb && sheet === 59)) { foundSheet = sheet; break; }
        }
      }
      if (foundSheet < 0) continue;
      // 三锚尝试（cs:74232-74246）：PlaceJunglePlant 锚=2×2 右下角 → placeBlock 左上角
      for (const ox of [0, -1, 1]) {
        if (this.attemptBulbAt(px + ox, py - 1, forceBulb)) return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /** AttemptToGeneratePlanteraBulbAt（cs:74250-74329）。锚 (i,j)=灯泡 2×2 右下角
   *  （底行 j 之下两列须为实心丛林草,cs:47820-47827）；2×2 须空或仅藤蔓/蘑菇/
   *  丛林植物 233（cs:47795-47812，可覆写）。forceBulb 档：4×4 邻域计分
   *  （泥/草 +1,其余活动格 -1,黑名单格直接 -1）≥12 才放,且清 4×4 + 外环转草 60。 */
  private attemptBulbAt(i: number, j: number, forceBulb: boolean): boolean {
    const st = this.host.store;
    // 底承:两列 j+1 行须实心丛林草
    if (!(st.inBounds(i - 1, j + 1) && st.inBounds(i, j + 1))) return false;
    if (!(this.solidTile(i - 1, j + 1) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i - 1, j + 1)) === 60)) return false;
    if (!(this.solidTile(i, j + 1) && sheetOf(this.typeAt(i, j + 1)) === 60)) return false;
    // 2×2 清场校验（藤 62 / 蘑菇植物 63-71 / 丛林植物 233 可覆写）
    for (let dx = -1; dx <= 0; dx++) {
      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 0; dy++) {
        const sh = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i + dx, j + dy));
        if (this.active(i + dx, j + dy)
          && sh !== 62 && sh !== 233 && !(sh >= 63 && sh <= 71)) return false;
      }
    }
    if (forceBulb) {
      // 计分（cs:74255-74296）：4×4 邻域泥/草 +1、其余活动 -1；黑名单即 -1
      let score = 0;
      outer:
      for (let k = -2; k < 2; k++) {
        for (let l = -2; l < 2; l++) {
          const sh = sheetOf(this.typeAt(i + k, j + l));
          if (sh === 59 || sh === 60) score++;
          else if (this.active(i + k, j + l)) score--;
          if (BURST_BLACKLIST.has(sh)) { score = -1; break outer; }
        }
      }
      if (score < 12) return false;
      // 清 4×4 + 外环转丛林草（cs:74298-74309）
      for (let m = -2; m < 2; m++) {
        for (let n = -2; n < 2; n++) {
          this.kill(i + m, j + n);
          if (m === -2 || m === 1 || n === -2 || n === 1) this.setType(i + m, j + n, S(60)!);
        }
      }
    }
    return this.placeBlock(i - 1, j - 1, 238, 2, 2);
  }

  // ================= 采样子分发（OvergroundTile cs:72066） =================

  private overgroundTile(i: number, j: number, wallDist: number): void {
    const st = this.host.store;
    const minI = Math.max(10, i - 1), maxI = Math.min(st.w - 10, i + 2);
    const minJ = Math.max(10, j - 1), maxJ = Math.min(st.h - 10, j + 2);
    if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) return;
```

```
      if (toDirt !== -1) {
        for (let k = minI; k < maxI; k++) {
          for (let l = minJ; l < maxJ; l++) {
```

```
    }
    this.openChest?.(chest as never);
    this.input.rightDown = false;
  }

  /** 拉杆/开关/计时器:右键 → Wiring.HitSwitch(Wiring.cs:163-252) */
  /** 拉杆/开关/计时器/起爆器:右键 → Wiring.hitSwitch(Wiring.cs:163-252 1:1)。
   *  hitSwitch 内按 sheet 分派帧翻转（开关 136 frameY 0↔18 / 计时器 144 注册冷却 /
   *  拉杆 132 等），再 tripWire 沿四色导线 BFS 传播信号触发沿线器件 */
  private toggleSwitch(x: number, y: number, type: number): void {
    void type;
    this.wiring?.hitSwitch(x, y);
    this.sfx.play('tink');
    this.input.rightDown = false;
  }

  // ================= 蜥蜴祭坛 237 / 永恒水晶座 466 / 传送晶塔 597 =================

  /** 蜥蜴祭坛右键（Player.cs:32303-32335 1:1）：条件门 !AnyNPCs(245) && hardMode
   *  && downedPlantBoss → 顺序扫背包消耗蜥蜴能量电池 1293（:32309-32319）→
   *  SoundID 15 Roar（:32328）→ NPC.SpawnOnPlayer(245)。
   *  条件不满足时原版**静默**（无提示文本——flag8=false 直接无事发生）。 */
  private useLihzahrdAltar(tx: number, ty: number): void {
    void tx; void ty;
    const w = this.world;
    // 条件门（:32307；原版静默——本作按惯例给反馈，偏差已记录）
    if (this.hasNpcId(245)) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.BossActive'));
      this.input.rightDown = false;
      return;
    }
    if (!w.flags.hardMode) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NeedHardMode'));
      this.input.rightDown = false;
      return;
    }
    if (!w.flags['downed_262']) {
      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NeedDownedPlant'));
      this.input.rightDown = false;
      return;
    }
    // 电池消耗（:32309-32319：inventory 顺序首个 type==1293 → stack-1 → 空则整格清空）
    if (!this.consumeVanillaItem(1293)) { this.input.rightDown = false; return; }
    this.sfx.playWav('roar');   // SoundID 15（:32328）
    this.spawnGolemFromAltar();
    // 成就：TEMPLE_RAIDER（WorldGen.cs:37641 event 22——祭坛使用成功）
    this.achievements.notifyProgressionEvent(22);
    this.input.rightDown = false;
  }

  /** 原版物品 id 的背包消耗（Player.ConsumeItem 语义的顺序扫描版：首格命中扣 1，
   *  空则清格——祭坛 1293 :32309-32319 / 水晶座 3828 :31968 同构） */
  private consumeVanillaItem(vid: number): boolean {
    const slots = this.player.inv.slots;
    for (let n = 0; n < slots.length; n++) {
      const it = slots[n];
      if (it && slotVanillaId(it.id) === vid) {
        it.stack--;
        if (it.stack <= 0) slots[n] = null;
        this.cb.onInventoryChanged?.();
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }

  /** 原版物品 id 的背包计数（HasItemInInventoryOrOpenVoidBag :31961 的背包段） */
  private countVanillaItem(vid: number): number {
    let n = 0;
    for (const it of this.player.inv.slots) {
      if (it && slotVanillaId(it.id) === vid) n += it.stack;
    }
    return n;
  }

  /** SpawnOnPlayer case 245（NPC.cs:81284-81330，落位计算在 world/BossSummonStations.ts）：
   *  NewNPC + 广播 Announcement.HasAwoken（:81325-81326；ChatColors.BossOrEvent）。 */
  private spawnGolemFromAltar(): void {
    const ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_237_lihzahrd_altar'];
    if (!ALTAR) return;
    const pcx = Math.floor(this.player.cx / TILE), pcy = Math.floor(this.player.cy / TILE);
```

```
18:  * 80% (only in Hardmode)
50:  * 80% (only in Hardmode)
108:**Demon Altars** and **Crimson Altars** are naturally-occurring [crafting stations](Crafting_station "Crafting station") found mainly in and around [chasms](Chasms.md "Chasms") in [The Corruption](The_Corruption.md "The Corruption") or [Crimson](The_Crimson.md "The Crimson"), and less frequently [underground](Underground.md "Underground"). They are used to craft [pre-Hardmode](Pre-Hardmode.md "Pre-Hardmode") [boss](Boss "Boss")-summoning items and the [Night's Edge](Night's_Edge.md "Night's Edge") as well as the [Void Bag](Void_Bag.md "Void Bag") and [Vault](Void_Vault.md "Void Vault"). Placed altars emit some light, making them easy to spot. They cannot be freed, picked up, or crafted. A Demon or Crimson Altar item will drop from the [Eye of Cthulhu](Eye_of_Cthulhu.md "Eye of Cthulhu") if no altars are present in the world. 
110:Once a world enters [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode"), they can be destroyed with any [hammer](Hammer "Hammer") that has 80% or greater hammer power. Hitting them with a hammer otherwise causes the player to lose half their [health](Health.md "Health") and will leave the altar unaffected. 
157:  * [](Crimtane_Ore.md "Crimtane Ore")[Crimtane Ore](Crimtane_Ore.md "Crimtane Ore")5
163:  * [](Demonite_Ore.md "Demonite Ore")[Demonite Ore](Demonite_Ore.md "Demonite Ore")5
226:  * [](Jungle_Spores.md "Jungle Spores")[Jungle Spores](Jungle_Spores.md "Jungle Spores")15

245:  * [](Jungle_Spores.md "Jungle Spores")[Jungle Spores](Jungle_Spores.md "Jungle Spores")8
256:## Hardmode
258:After the [Wall of Flesh](Wall_of_Flesh.md "Wall of Flesh") has been defeated in a world, altars can be destroyed with any tool that has a hammer power of at least 80% (typically the [Pwnhammer](Pwnhammer.md "Pwnhammer")). Each destroyed altar will spawn a [Wraith](Wraith.md "Wraith") and causes one type of Hardmode [ore](Ore "Ore") to appear in the world, with the appropriate [status message](Status_message "Status message") displaying in the chat: _" Your world has been blessed with <name of ore>!"_
260:  1. First altar: [](Cobalt_Ore.md "Cobalt Ore")[Cobalt Ore](Cobalt_Ore.md "Cobalt Ore") or [](Palladium_Ore.md "Palladium Ore")[Palladium Ore](Palladium_Ore.md "Palladium Ore"), chosen randomly.
261:  2. Second altar: [](Mythril_Ore.md "Mythril Ore")[Mythril Ore](Mythril_Ore.md "Mythril Ore") or [](Orichalcum_Ore.md "Orichalcum Ore")[Orichalcum Ore](Orichalcum_Ore.md "Orichalcum Ore"), chosen randomly.
262:  3. Third altar: [](Adamantite_Ore.md "Adamantite Ore")[Adamantite Ore](Adamantite_Ore.md "Adamantite Ore") or [](Titanium_Ore.md "Titanium Ore")[Titanium Ore](Titanium_Ore.md "Titanium Ore"), chosen randomly.
264:Players will be able to find these new ores scattered across the Underground [layer](Layer "Layer") to near-[Underworld](Underworld "Underworld") levels, with them replacing most blocks created during world generation. They will spawn at random locations in varying quantity, in places explored and unexplored alike. _For technical details about the ore generation, see[Ores § Generation](Ores#Generation_2 "Ores")._
266:  * Destroying more altars will never spawn Hardmode ore types beyond the three that were randomly assigned to the world during the initial round of destroyed altars. A world with Palladium Ore deposits will never spawn Cobalt Ore deposits, or vice versa (with the exception of [drunk worlds](Drunk_world "Drunk world")).
267:  * Which ores are generated is not determined until the first, second or third altar is smashed. For example, it is not determined if the world has Cobalt or Palladium until the first altar has been smashed. The same goes for Mythril/Orichalcum with the second altar, and Adamantite/Titanium for the third.
268:  * Subsequent altars destroyed will begin the cycle again, though the fourth to sixth altars will spawn half as much ore as the first ones, the seventh to ninth will spawn only one third of the ore of the first ones, and so forth. So, the nth cycle will only generate one-nth as much ore as the first one. For example, destroying the 19th altar (seventh cycle) would yield one-seventh of Cobalt/Palladium Ore compared to the first altar. Destroying 12 altars will roughly double the ore provided by the first three; tripling that original allotment would require destroying 33 altars. Notice that although each subsequent destroyed altar will yield less ore, the total amount is [unbounded](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/en:Harmonic_series_\(mathematics\) "wikipedia:en:Harmonic series \(mathematics\)") (except by the number of altars in a world).
269:  * No matter how many altars are destroyed, ore rarity remains set with Cobalt/Palladium being the most common, followed by Mythril/Orichalcum, then Adamantite/Titanium as the rarest.
270:  * Each time an altar is destroyed, one or two [Wraiths](Wraith.md "Wraith") spawn, regardless of the [time of day](Day_and_night_cycle.md "Day and night cycle").
271:  * Additionally, smashing an altar will allow [mechanical bosses](Mechanical_bosses.md "Mechanical bosses") (if said boss has not been defeated before it will spawn with a 1/10 (10%) chance each night) and random [Pirate Invasions](Pirate_Invasion.md "Pirate Invasion") (if all [the criteria](Pirate_Invasion#Summoning "Pirate Invasion") are met) to spawn naturally.
278:  * Altars destroyed prior to [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode") will have no effect.
281:    * The game will make 1000 attempts to generate each Altar this way before moving on. It is possible that no Altars will generate outside of the Corruption/Crimson.
```

```
48:The **Lihzahrd Altar** is a [furniture](Furniture.md "Furniture") item used for summoning [Golem](Golem.md "Golem") with [Lihzahrd Power Cells](Lihzahrd_Power_Cell.md "Lihzahrd Power Cell"). Upon pressing [`⚷ Open / Activate`](Game_controls.md "Game controls") on it, one Lihzahrd Power Cell is consumed from the player's inventory and Golem is instantly summoned. The Lihzahrd Altar is always found [naturally placed](Category%3ANaturally_generated "Category:Naturally generated") in the final chamber of the [Jungle Temple](Jungle_Temple.md "Jungle Temple"). 
50:Like [Lihzahrd Bricks](Lihzahrd_Brick.md "Lihzahrd Brick"), the Lihzahrd Altar is [impervious to explosions](Explosion-proof_objects.md "Explosion-proof objects") and can only be removed with the [Picksaw](Picksaw.md "Picksaw"), [Laser Drill](Laser_Drill.md "Laser Drill"), or [Luminite Pickaxes](Luminite_Pickaxes.md "Luminite Pickaxes"). Once picked up, it can be placed anywhere and can be used to summon Golem in any area at any time, as long as the world is in [Hardmode](Hardmode.md "Hardmode") with [Plantera](Plantera.md "Plantera") defeated. 
54:  * Pressing [`⚷ Open / Activate`](Game_controls.md "Game controls") on the Lihzahrd Altar with a Lihzahrd Power Cell in the inventory will automatically consume it. The Lihzahrd Power Cell does not need to be held.
55:  * [Paints](Paints.md "Paints") can only affect the Lihzahrd Altar tiles and cannot affect the floating symbol above the altar.
56:  * If no Jungle Temple blocks are placed in the world, a Lihzahrd Altar can be dropped by Plantera. _See[Plantera's Jungle Temple drops](Plantera's_Jungle_Temple_drops.md "Plantera's Jungle Temple drops") for details._
```

```
Deviations:
A. 🔀/❌ CRITICAL: periodic bulb + life fruit spawn placed in the OVERGROUND half of grassGrowth (WorldEvolution.ts:712-733) but vanilla has it in the UNDERGROUND half (WorldGen.cs:74471-74560, proven by `flag` scope: declared 74429 inside if(underground), referenced at 74479; block closes at 74575-74576 `return; }`, surface switch starts 74578). Consequence: bulbs/life fruit only spawn on surface jungle grass samples (y < worldSurface), never in the underground jungle. Also the implementation's surface case 60 comment misattributes cs:74821-74886 as containing bulb+life fruit — it doesn't.
   Also minor: vanilla allows a single sample to attempt bulb then life fruit then 233 (no else-chain); implementation uses else-if (bulb attempt suppresses life fruit/233). And vanilla life fruit chance 1/40 classic / 1/30 expert, spacing 60/50 — impl fixed 1/30 & 60.

Temple freely enterable pre-Plantera; only mitigations are wiring gates + altar's downed_262 gate.

E. ⚠️ Mech summon items consumed unconditionally even if summonBoss fails (boss already present) — Game.ts:4484-4490; contradicts vanilla SummonItemCheck (Player.cs:41514-41526/51441-51447) and their own fix for 70/1331/560 at Game.ts:4466-4476.
F. ⚠️ minor numeric: smashAltar vein count uses Math.floor(veins) vs vanilla loop `k < num3` (double) → ceil(veins) iterations (one fewer vein on average); OreRunner frequency/length share one roll r vs two independent Next(5,10); cobalt ×1.05 vs 1.0499999523, alt ×0.9 vs 0.8999999761 (negligible). Game.ts:5072-5080.

H. Minor: Golem altar gives toasts when conditions unmet (vanilla silent) — documented deviation, Game.ts:6629-6640.
I. HardmodeStart RNG: new RNG(world.seed) stream instead of genRand mid-state — documented structural equivalence (HardmodePass.ts:12-14). Not a bug.
```

```
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.DataStructures;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent.Events;

public class CultistRitual
{
	public const int delayStart = 86400;

	public const int respawnDelay = 43200;

	private const int timePerCultist = 3600;

	private const int recheckStart = 600;

	public static int delay;

	public static int recheck;

	public static void UpdateTime()
	{
		if (Main.netMode == 1)
		{
			return;
		}
		delay -= Main.dayRate;
		if (delay < 0)
		{
			delay = 0;
		}
		recheck -= Main.dayRate;
		if (recheck < 0)
		{
			recheck = 0;
		}
		if (delay == 0 && recheck == 0)
		{
			recheck = 600;
			if (NPC.AnyDanger())
			{
				recheck *= 6;
			}
			else
			{
				TrySpawning(Main.dungeonX, Main.dungeonY);
			}
		}
	}

	public static void CultistSlain()
	{
		delay -= 3600;
	}

	public static void TabletDestroyed()
	{
		delay = 43200;
	}

	public static bool TrySpawning(int x, int y, bool force = false)
	{
		if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= Main.maxTilesX || y >= Main.maxTilesY)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!force && (WorldGen.PlayerLOS(x - 6, y) || WorldGen.PlayerLOS(x + 6, y)))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!CheckRitual(x, y, force))
		{
			return false;
		}
		NPC.NewNPC(new EntitySource_WorldEvent(), x * 16 + 8, (y - 4) * 16 - 8, 437);
		return true;
	}

	private static bool CheckRitual(int x, int y, bool force = false)
	{
		if (!force && (delay != 0 || !Main.hardMode || !NPC.downedGolemBoss || !NPC.downedBoss3))
		{
			return false;
		}

			return false;
		}

			return false;
		}
		Vector2 center = new Vector2(x * 16 + 8, y * 16 - 64 - 8 - 27);
		Point[] spawnPoints = null;
		if (!CheckFloor(center, out spawnPoints))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public static bool CheckFloor(Vector2 Center, out Point[] spawnPoints)
	{
		Point[] array = new Point[4];
		int num = 0;
		Point point = Center.ToTileCoordinates();

			if (i == -1 || i == 1)

		}
		if (num != 4)
		{
			spawnPoints = null;
			return false;
		}
		spawnPoints = array;
		return true;
	}
```

**📎 ToolResult**

```
  lanternsCanStart(ctx: LanternCtx): boolean {
    return !ctx.spawnMeteor && !ctx.bloodMoon && !ctx.pumpkinMoon && !ctx.snowMoon
      && ctx.invasionType === 0 && ctx.moonLordCountdown === 0 && !ctx.bossActive();
  }

  /** UpdateTime（:128-153，每 tick）：真灯笼不再可续即提前收场；
   *  天空激活/关闭由渲染层按 LanternsUp 派生（LanternSky._opacity ±0.01/帧），
   *  原 _wasLanternNight 边沿只服务 SkyManager 调度，此处仅保留态位 */
  updateTime(ctx: LanternCtx): void {
    if (this.genuineLanterns && !this.lanternsCanPersist(ctx)) {
      this.genuineLanterns = false;
    }
    this.wasLanternNight = this.lanternsUp;
  }

  /** WorldClear（:120-126）：读图重置（运行时态不进存档） */
  worldClear(): void {
    this.manualLanterns = false;
    this.genuineLanterns = false;
    this.nextNightIsLanternNight = false;
    this.lanternNightsOnCooldown = 0;
    this.wasLanternNight = false;
  }

  /** OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime（NPC.cs:79569-79601 switch 1:1）：
   *  default / 10（月总）/ 16-17-18（三王）→ 预约次夜灯笼夜；
   *  4（哀木）/ 21（圣诞树）/ 22（圣诞坦克）→ 不预约。
   *  id 10 的 CreditsRoll 与 16-18 的砸祭坛后世花球茎重生成
   *  （WorldGen.GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated）为各自子系统，未随本表触发。 */
  onGameEventCleared(gameEventId: number): void {
    switch (gameEventId) {
      case 4:
      case 21:
      case 22:
        return;
      default:
        this.nextNightIsLanternNight = true;
        break;
    }
  }
}

/** 单例（对应原版静态类） */
export const LanternNight = new LanternNightState();
```

````
Confirmed: `onGameEventCleared` unconditionally sets `nextNightIsLanternNight = true` — no first-time guard. The function is documented as "OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime" but lacks the "for the first time" guard. In vanilla, SetEventFlagCleared(ref flag, id) only calls OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime when the flag transitions from false to true (NPC.cs:79560-79568: `eventFlag = true; if (eventFlag != flag) OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime(id)` — wait, actually looking at the earlier read of NPC.cs:79560-79568:

```
eventFlag = true;
if (eventFlag != flag) { OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime(gameEventId); }
```

Hmm, this is inside SetEventFlagCleared where `flag` is the ref'd flag's ORIGINAL value passed... Let me re-read: 

```csharp
public static void SetEventFlagCleared(ref bool eventFlag, int gameEventId)
{
    bool flag = eventFlag;      // presumably
    eventFlag = true;
    if (eventFlag != flag) { OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime(gameEventId); }
}
```

From my earlier read of 79560-79568:
```
				eventFlag = true;
				if (eventFlag != flag)
				{
					OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime(gameEventId);
				}
```
Yes — fires only on false→true transition (first time). 

So in the implementation, every re-kill of a boss (Destroyer again, Plantera again, etc.) schedules another lantern night — deviation from vanilla "first time only". This affects the hardmode chain (re-farming mechs/Plantera/Golem would trigger lantern nights repeatedly). It's a 🔀 finding. Though — the memory notes say "lantern night" domain was ported; maybe they consider this acceptable. But the function name itself claims 1:1 with "ForTheFirstTime" — so it's a genuine mismatch worth reporting as ⚠️/🔀.

Also the bulb burst: implementation gates on flags all-true, which stays true after first kill, so re-killing the third mech (e.g., all three re-summoned and re-killed) would call planteraBulbBurst() again — vanilla wouldn't (first-time only). But since a bulb already exists, the burst would place a second bulb (vanilla: guaranteed bulb only once). Minor.
````
